        uint256 value=_5 - subtractedValue)

def permit(address owner, address spender, uint256 value, uint256 deadline, uint8 v, bytes32 r, bytes32 s): # not payable
  if deadline < block.timestamp:
      revert with 'DEADLINE_EXPIRED'
  if nonces[address(owner)] + 1 < nonces[address(owner)]:
      revert with 'MATH_ADD_OVERFLOW'
  _3 = nonces[address(owner)]
  nonces[address(owner)]++

  call address(stor42B2).unknown804e5eb3(?) with:
       gas gas_remaining
      args this.address, sha3(0x6e71edae12b1b97f4d1f60370fef10105fa2faae0126114a169c64845d6126c9, address(owner), address(spender), value, _3, deadline)
  if not ext_call.success:
      revert with ext_call.return_data[0 len return_data.size]
  require return_data.size >= 32
  if ext_code.size(address(owner)) <= 0:
      if s > 0x7fffffffffffffffffffffffffffffff5d576e7357a4501ddfe92f46681b20a0:
          revert with 'ECDSA: invalid signature \'s\' value'
      mem[416] = 0
      mem[448] = ext_call.return_data[0]
      signer = ecrecover(mem[448], uint8(v), r, s) # precompiled
      mem[416 len min(32, return_data.size)] = ext_call.return_data[0 len min(32, return_data.size)]
      if not ecrecover.result:
          revert with ext_call.return_data[0 len return_data.size]
      if not mem[428 len 20]:
          revert with 'ECDSA: invalid signature'
      _42 = mem[428 len 20] == address(owner)
  else:
      static call address(owner).isValidSignature(bytes32, bytes) with:
              gas gas_remaining
             args ext_call.return_data[0], 64, 65, r, s, v << 248
      mem[741 len min(32, return_data.size)] = ext_call.return_data[0 len min(32, return_data.size)]
      _39 = ext_call.success == 1 and return_data.size == 32 and concat(ext_call.return_data[0 len min(32, return_data.size)], mem[min(32, return_data.size) + 741 len -min(32, return_data.size) + 32])
      _42 = _39 == 0x1626ba7e00000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000
  if not _42:
      revert with 'INVALID_SIGNATURE'
  if not address(owner):
      revert with 'APPROVE_FROM_ZERO_ADDR'
  if not address(spender):

      revert with 'BALANCE_EXCEEDED'
